TL;DR: 本文提出Token-Superposition Training (TST)方法，通过将多个连续token叠加为"袋"并使用多热交叉熵目标训练，在不修改模型架构/优化器/分词器等组件的情况下，显著提升预训练数据吞吐量，在10B参数MoE模型上实现最高2.5倍预训练时间缩减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型预训练成本高昂且效率低下，现有加速方法需要对并行策略、优化器等组件进行复杂且侵入性的修改，缺乏简单即插即用的高效训练方案。

Method: TST包含两阶段：(i)叠加阶段——将多个连续token组合成一个"袋"，采用多热交叉熵(MCE)目标进行训练；(ii)恢复阶段——回退到标准训练方式。该方法无需修改并行策略、优化器、分词器、数据或模型架构。

Result: 在270M和600M参数规模上充分验证，并在3B和10B A1B混合专家模型上进一步验证。在等损失条件下，TST在10B A1B规模上实现最高2.5倍预训练时间缩减，且持续优于基线的损失和下游评估指标。

Conclusion: TST是一种简单高效的预训练加速方法，具有高鲁棒性，在多种不同设置下均表现优异，能显著降低大语言模型预训练的计算成本。

Abstract: Pre-training of Large Language Models is often prohibitively expensive and inefficient at scale, requiring complex and invasive modifications in order to achieve high data throughput. In this work, we present Token-Superposition Training (TST), a simple drop-in method that significantly improves the data throughput per FLOPs during pre-training without modifying the parallelism, optimizer, tokenizer, data, or model architecture. TST is done in two phases: (i) A highly efficient superposition phase where we combine many contiguous tokens into one bag and train using a multi-hot cross-entropy (MCE) objective, and (ii) a recovery phase where we revert back to standard training. We extensively evaluate TST on the scale of 270M and 600M parameters and validate on 3B and a 10B A1B mixture of experts model, demonstrating that it is highly robust in different settings. Ultimately, TST consistently outperforms baseline loss and downstream evaluations, and under equal-loss settings, TST yields up to a 2.5x reduction in total pre-training time at the 10B A1B scale.

</details>


### [146] [Continuous Latent Diffusion Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06548)
*Hongcan Guo,Qinyu Zhao,Yian Zhao,Shen Nie,Rui Zhu,Qiushan Guo,Feng Wang,Tao Yang,Hengshuang Zhao,Guoqiang Wei,Yan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Cola DLM是一种层次化潜在扩散语言模型，通过文本VAE、块因果DiT和条件解码实现文本生成，采用离散文本与连续潜空间的分解策略，在约2B参数规模下验证了其扩展行为和生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归范式虽成功但固定从左到右生成并非必要；现有替代方案在生成效率、可扩展表示学习与全局语义建模之间难以兼顾。

Method: 采用三阶段架构：Text VAE学习稳定的文本到潜空间映射；块因果DiT在连续潜空间中建模全局语义先验；条件解码生成文本。扩散过程执行潜先验运输而非token级观测恢复，将全局语义组织与局部文本实现分离。

Result: 在4个研究问题、8个基准、严格匹配的约2B参数自回归与LLaDA基线上进行了实验，并展示了约2000 EFLOPs的扩展曲线，验证了Cola DLM的有效配置与强扩展行为。

Conclusion: 层次化连续潜先验建模可作为严格token级语言建模的有原则替代方案，生成质量与扩展行为可能比似然更能反映模型能力，并为跨离散文本与连续模态的统一建模提供了具体路径。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable success under the autoregressive paradigm, yet high-quality text generation need not be tied to a fixed left-to-right order. Existing alternatives still struggle to jointly achieve generation efficiency, scalable representation learning, and effective global semantic modeling. We propose Cola DLM, a hierarchical latent diffusion language model that frames text generation through hierarchical information decomposition. Cola DLM first learns a stable text-to-latent mapping with a Text VAE, then models a global semantic prior in continuous latent space with a block-causal DiT, and finally generates text through conditional decoding. From a unified Markov-path perspective, its diffusion process performs latent prior transport rather than token-level observation recovery, thereby separating global semantic organization from local textual realization. This design yields a more flexible non-autoregressive inductive bias, supports semantic compression and prior fitting in continuous space, and naturally extends to other continuous modalities. Through experiments spanning 4 research questions, 8 benchmarks, strictly matched ~2B-parameter autoregressive and LLaDA baselines, and scaling curves up to about 2000 EFLOPs, we identify an effective overall configuration of Cola DLM and verify its strong scaling behavior for text generation. Taken together, the results establish hierarchical continuous latent prior modeling as a principled alternative to strictly token-level language modeling, where generation quality and scaling behavior may better reflect model capability than likelihood, while also suggesting a concrete path toward unified modeling across discrete text and continuous modalities.

</details>


### [147] [Long Context Pre-Training with Lighthouse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06554)
*Bowen Peng,Subho Ghosh,Jeffrey Quesnelle*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Lighthouse Attention，一种用于因果Transformer训练的分层选择注意力算法，通过对称压缩/解压缩序列将SDPA的计算复杂度降至亚二次，并采用两阶段训练（先使用Lighthouse Attention预训练，再短时恢复全注意力）以加速训练并降低最终损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练因果Transformer时，缩放点积注意力（SDPA）的二次时间和内存开销成为极端序列长度下的瓶颈。

Method: 提出Lighthouse Attention，一种仅用于训练的分层对称选择注意力算法：包含亚二次的分层预处理（自适应压缩）和后处理（解压缩）步骤；对称压缩策略同时池化查询、键和值并保持因果性，提升并行度；两阶段训练——大部分时间用Lighthouse Attention预训练，最后短时恢复全注意力。

Result: 小规模LLM预训练实验表明，在匹配所有其他设置的情况下，与全注意力训练相比，该方法实现了更快的总训练时间和恢复阶段后更低的最终损失。

Conclusion: Lighthouse Attention通过训练时替换SDPA并在最后短时恢复的方式，有效降低了因果Transformer训练的复杂度，加速训练且不牺牲最终性能。

Abstract: Training causal transformers at extreme sequence lengths is bottlenecked by the quadratic time and memory of scaled dot-product attention (SDPA). In this work, we propose Lighthouse Attention, a training-only symmetrical selection-based hierarchical attention algorithm that wraps around ordinary SDPA and can be easily removed towards the end of the training. Our hierarchical selection is also gradient-free, which exempts us from dealing with a complicated and potentially inefficient backward pass kernel. Our contribution is three-fold: (i) A subquadratic hierarchical pre- and post-processing step that does adaptive compression and decompression of the sequence. (ii) A symmetrical compression strategy that pools queries, keys and values at the same time, while preserving left-to-right causality, which greatly improves parallelism. (iii) A two stage training approach which we pre-train for the majority of the time with Lighthouse Attention and recover a full attention model at the end with a short training. We run preliminary small scale LLM pre-training experiments that show the effectiveness of our method compared to full attention training with all other settings matched, where we achieve a faster total training time and lower final loss after the recovery phase. Full code is available at: https://github.com/ighoshsubho/lighthouse-attention

</details>


### [148] [Automated Clinical Report Generation for Remote Cognitive Remediation: Comparing Knowledge-Engineered Templates and LLMs in Low-Resource Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06594)
*Yongxin Zhou,Fabien Ringeval,François Portet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在低资源环境下（无参考报告）为远程认知康复训练自动生成临床报告的方法。比较了基于规则的模板系统（编码言语治疗领域知识）与基于GPT-4的零样本LLM方法。八位言语治疗师从九个维度评估后显示：模板系统在流畅性、连贯性和结果呈现上得分更高，GPT-4输出更简洁，但所有差异均未达统计显著性。基于反馈提出了八项临床报告系统设计建议，贡献了可复现的临床NLG评估方法论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知康复治疗需求增长而言语治疗师资源有限，远程康复工具产生的海量交互数据难以人工审核，亟需自动化临床报告生成以减轻临床人员负担。

Method: 采用两种方法：1）基于规则的模板系统，编码言语治疗领域知识为显式决策规则和验证模板；2）零样本GPT-4方法，生成更流畅简洁的输出。两种系统使用相同的预提取且经专家验证的结构化变量，通过八位言语治疗师使用九项标准问卷进行对比评估。

Result: 模板系统在流畅性、连贯性和结果呈现上得分更高，GPT-4输出更简洁。所有维度上的方向性差异一致，但经多重比较校正后均未达到统计显著性。

Conclusion: 临床可靠性与语言质量之间存在明确权衡。基于评估者反馈提出八项设计建议。贡献了一个结合专家启发、分类驱动生成和多维人工评估的可复现方法论，为生成式AI在医疗领域的负责任应用提供了参考。

Abstract: The growing demand for cognitive remediation therapy, combined with limited speech therapist availability, has accelerated the adoption of remote rehabilitation tools. These systems generate large volumes of interaction data that are difficult for clinicians to review efficiently. This paper investigates automated clinical report generation for avatar-guided, home-based cognitive remediation sessions in a low-resource setting with no reference reports. We present and compare two approaches: (1) a rule-based template system encoding speech therapy domain knowledge as explicit decision rules and validated templates, ensuring clinical reliability and traceability; and (2) a zero-shot LLM-based approach (GPT-4) aimed at more fluent and concise output. Both systems use identical pre-extracted, expert-validated structured variables, enabling a controlled factual comparison. Outputs were evaluated by eight speech therapists and final-year students using a nine-criterion questionnaire. Results reveal a clear trade-off between clinical reliability and linguistic quality. The template-based system scored higher on fluidity, coherence, and results presentation, while GPT-4 produced more concise output. Directional differences are consistent across evaluation dimensions, though no comparison reached statistical significance after correction, reflecting the scale constraints of expert clinical evaluation. Based on evaluator feedback, we derive eight design recommendations for clinical reporting systems in remote rehabilitation settings. More broadly, this work contributes a replicable methodology combining expert elicitation, taxonomy-driven generation, and multi-dimensional human evaluation for clinical NLG in low-resource settings, and illustrates how controlled comparisons can inform the responsible adoption of generative AI in healthcare.

</details>


### [149] [UniSD: Towards a Unified Self-Distillation Framework for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06597)
*Yiqiao Jin,Yiyang Wang,Lucheng Fu,Yijia Xiao,Yinyi Luo,Haoxin Liu,B. Aditya Prakash,Josiah Hester,Jindong Wang,Srijan Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出UniSD统一框架系统研究自蒸馏（SD）在自回归大语言模型中的应用，整合了多教师一致、EMA教师稳定、token级对比学习、特征匹配和散度裁剪等互补机制。通过跨6个基准、6个模型和3个模型族的实验，揭示了SD何时优于静态模仿、各组件贡献及交互关系。基于这些洞察构建的UniSDfull集成管道相比基础模型提升+5.4分，比最强基线提升+2.8分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自蒸馏（SD）为无需强外部教师的大模型适配提供了有前景的路径，但自回归LLM中的SD面临挑战：自生成轨迹自由形式、正确性依赖任务、合理理由仍可能提供不稳定/不可靠监督。现有方法主要检查孤立的设计选择，其有效性、角色和交互关系尚不清晰。

Method: 提出UniSD统一框架，集成五种互补机制：多教师一致性（multi-teacher agreement）、EMA教师稳定化（EMA teacher stabilization）、token级对比学习（token-level contrastive learning）、特征匹配（feature matching）和散度裁剪（divergence clipping）。在6个基准、6个模型（来自3个模型族）上系统评估。

Result: UniSD揭示了SD何时优于静态模仿、哪些组件驱动增益及组件间如何跨任务交互。构建的UniSDfull集成管道实现最强整体性能：相比基础模型提升+5.4分，相比最强基线提升+2.8分。

Conclusion: 自蒸馏是一种实用且可控的高效LLM适配方法，无需依赖更强的外部教师即可实现显著性能提升。

Abstract: Self-distillation (SD) offers a promising path for adapting large language models (LLMs) without relying on stronger external teachers. However, SD in autoregressive LLMs remains challenging because self-generated trajectories are free-form, correctness is task-dependent, and plausible rationales can still provide unstable or unreliable supervision. Existing methods mainly examine isolated design choices, leaving their effectiveness, roles, and interactions unclear. In this paper, we propose UniSD, a unified framework to systematically study self-distillation. UniSD integrates complementary mechanisms that address supervision reliability, representation alignment, and training stability, including multi-teacher agreement, EMA teacher stabilization, token-level contrastive learning, feature matching, and divergence clipping. Across six benchmarks and six models from three model families, UniSD reveals when self-distillation improves over static imitation, which components drive the gains, and how these components interact across tasks. Guided by these insights, we construct UniSDfull, an integrated pipeline that combines complementary components and achieves the strongest overall performance, improving over the base model by +5.4 points and the strongest baseline by +2.8 points. Extensive evaluation highlights self-distillation as a practical and steerable approach for efficient LLM adaptation without stronger external teachers.

</details>


### [150] [Algospeak, Hiding in the Open: The Trade-off Between Legible Meaning and Detection Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06619)
*Jan Fillies,Ronald E. Robertson,Jeffrey Hancock*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文形式化了Algospeak（算法语言）的共演化动态，提出“多数可理解调制”（MUM）概念，并构建了可复现的框架和数据集（700条调制项），通过COVID-19虚假信息案例验证了可检测性与可理解性之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型同时承担内容生成与审核角色，导致Algospeak（规避检测的语言策略）与检测器之间的共演化加剧，但缺乏对背后动态的形式化理解与量化分析框架。

Method: 基于联合行动模型形式化Algospeak动态；引入多数可理解调制（MUM）概念；基于现有分类法构建含5个调制级别、7种策略、700条调制项的可复现框架；使用7种语言模型进行意义恢复（可理解性）与分类（可检测性）的两阶段评估，通过曲线拟合估计MUM阈值。

Result: 揭示了可理解性与调制程度之间的特征关系，通过曲线拟合估算了MUM阈值，并实现了跨策略和跨模型的敏感性分析。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解Algospeak背后的动态奠定了基础，并提供了可复用的框架、数据集和实验设置。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) increasingly mediate both content generation and moderation, linguistic evasion strategies known as Algospeak have intensified the coevolution between evaders and detectors. This research formalizes the underlying dynamics grounded in a joint action model: when Algospeak increases, detectability and understandability decrease. Further, the concept of Majority Understandable Modulation (MUM) is introduced and defined as the modulation level at which additional evasive alteration increases detector evasion but loses comprehension for the majority of recipients. To empirically probe this trade-off, we introduce a reproducible framework that can be used to create meaning-preserving, Algospeak-style variants, based on an existing taxonomy and with tunable modulation levels. Using COVID-19 disinformation as a first proof-by-example setting, we construct a reference dataset of 700 modulated items, drawn from twenty base sentences across five modulation levels and seven strategies. We then run two linked evaluations with seven different language models: one testing for interpretation through meaning recovery and one for disinformation detection through classification. Curve fitting over modulation levels yields an estimate of the Majority Understandable Modulation threshold and enables sensitivity analyses across strategies and models, see Figure 1. Results reveal the characteristic relationships between understandability and modulation. This study lays the groundwork for understanding the dynamics behind Algospeak and provides the framework, dataset, and experimental setups described.

</details>


### [151] [Cited but Not Verified: Parsing and Evaluating Source Attribution in LLM Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06635)
*Hailey Onweller,Elias Lumer,Austin Huber,Pia Ramchandani,Vamse Kumar Subbiah,Corey Feld*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出首个基于AST解析器的引用来源归因评估框架，从链接可用性、内容相关性和事实准确性三个维度评估LLM生成报告中的内联引用。实验表明，最强模型链接有效性>94%、相关性>80%，但事实准确性仅39-77%；且随着检索工具调用次数增加（2→150次），事实准确性平均下降约42%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成的深度研究报告中的引用无法可靠验证，自引用方法存在偏差风险，RAG方法无法验证源可访问性、相关性和事实一致性，缺乏系统性的引用评估框架。

Method: 提出基于可复现AST解析器的引用评估框架，通过检索实际引用内容实现闭环验证。从三个维度评估：Link Works（URL可访问性）、Relevant Content（主题对齐度）、Fact Check（事实准确性）。使用基于评分的LLM-as-a-judge评估器，经人工审核校准。对14个闭源和开源LLM进行基准测试，并进行消融实验分析研究深度的影响。

Result: 最强模型链接有效性>94%、相关性>80%，但事实准确性仅39-77%；不到一半的开源模型能一次性成功生成带引用的报告。消融实验显示，工具调用从2次增加到150次时，两个前沿模型的事实准确性平均下降约42%。

Conclusion: 表面引用质量与事实可靠性之间存在严重脱节，更多检索不会带来更准确的引用，本框架为评估这一脱节提供了基础设施。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) power deep research agents that synthesize information from hundreds of web sources into cited reports, yet these citations cannot be reliably verified. Current approaches either trust models to self-cite accurately, risking bias, or employ retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) that does not validate source accessibility, relevance, or factual consistency. We introduce the first source attribution evaluation framework that uses a reproducible AST parser to extract and evaluate inline citations from LLM-generated Markdown reports at scale. Unlike methods that verify claims in isolation, our framework closes the loop by retrieving the actual cited content, enabling human or model evaluators to judge each citation against its source. Citations are evaluated along three dimensions. (1) Link Works verifies URL accessibility, (2) Relevant Content measures topical alignment, and (3) Fact Check validates factual accuracy against source content. We benchmark 14 closed-source and open-source LLMs across three evaluation dimensions using rubric-based LLM-as-a-judge evaluators calibrated through human review. Our results reveal that even the strongest frontier models maintain link validity above 94% and relevance above 80%, yet achieve only 39-77% factual accuracy, while fewer than half of open-source models successfully generate cited reports in a one-shot setting. Ablation studies on research depth show that Fact Check accuracy drops by approximately 42% on average across two frontier models as tool calls scale from 2 to 150, demonstrating that more retrieval does not produce more accurate citations. These findings reveal a critical disconnect between surface-level citation quality and factual reliability, and our framework provides the evaluation infrastructure to assess the disconnect.

</details>


### [152] [StraTA: Incentivizing Agentic Reinforcement Learning with Strategic Trajectory Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06642)
*Xiangyuan Xue,Yifan Zhou,Zidong Wang,Shengji Tang,Philip Torr,Wanli Ouyang,Lei Bai,Zhenfei Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StraTA通过引入轨迹级策略抽象和分层GRPO强化学习框架，在ALFWorld、WebShop和SciWorld等长期决策任务中显著提升了样本效率和最终性能，分别达到了93.1%、84.2%和63.5%的成功率/得分，超越了强基线和前沿闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体方法多为纯反应式，在长程轨迹中弱化了探索和信用分配，导致长期决策优化困难。

Method: 提出Strategic Trajectory Abstraction (StraTA)，从初始任务状态采样紧凑策略，以此条件化后续动作，并采用分层GRPO风格 rollout 设计联合训练策略生成与动作执行，辅以多样化策略rollout和关键自我判断机制。

Result: 在ALFWorld上达到93.1%成功率，WebShop上84.2%，SciWorld上63.5%总体得分，超越前沿闭源模型。

Conclusion: StraTA通过显式引入轨迹级策略抽象，有效改善了长程决策中的探索与信用分配问题，验证了层次化强化学习框架在LLM智能体训练中的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as interactive agents, but optimizing them for long-horizon decision making remains difficult because current methods are largely purely reactive, which weakens both exploration and credit assignment over extended trajectories. In this work, we present Strategic Trajectory Abstraction (StraTA), a simple framework that introduces an explicit trajectory-level strategy into agentic reinforcement learning (RL). StraTA samples a compact strategy from the initial task state, conditions subsequent actions on that strategy, and trains strategy generation and action execution jointly with a hierarchical GRPO-style rollout design, further enhanced by diverse strategy rollout and critical self-judgment. Experiments on ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld show that StraTA consistently improves both sample efficiency and final performance over strong baselines. StraTA reaches success rates of 93.1% on ALFWorld and 84.2% on WebShop. On SciWorld, StraTA attains a 63.5% overall score, outperforming frontier closed-source models.

</details>


### [153] [EMO: Pretraining Mixture of Experts for Emergent Modularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06663)
*Ryan Wang,Akshita Bhagia,Sewon Min*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EMO通过文档级专家共享约束，在预训练中实现专家自然聚类为语义领域，支持独立使用子集专家（仅用25%专家损失1%性能），解决了标准MoE在领域子集推理时性能严重下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型通常以 monolithic 方式部署，即使应用仅需狭窄能力子集（如代码、数学）也必须加载完整模型。标准MoE虽理论上可激活部分专家，但实践中对特定领域限制推理专家子集会导致严重性能下降，限制了其在内存受限场景下的实用性。

Method: 提出EMO，核心思想是鼓励相似域（domain）的token依赖相似专家。具体约束：同一文档内的token只能从共享的专家池中选择，不同文档可使用不同专家池。这一简单约束利用文档边界，在预训练中自然形成领域相干（coherent）的专家分组。

Result: 预训练1B激活/14B总参数的EMO（1T tokens）：(1) 全模型性能与标准MoE相当；(2) 选择性使用25%专家仅损失1%绝对性能，12.5%专家损失3%，而标准MoE在此设置下完全失效；(3) 专家子集在语义层面（如数学、代码领域）呈现专业化，而非标准MoE的低级句法专业化。

Conclusion: EMO展示了大型稀疏模型实现模块化、内存高效部署的可行路径，为可组合架构开辟了新机会。

Abstract: Large language models are typically deployed as monolithic systems, requiring the full model even when applications need only a narrow subset of capabilities, e.g., code, math, or domain-specific knowledge. Mixture-of-Experts (MoEs) seemingly offer a potential alternative by activating only a subset of experts per input, but in practice, restricting inference to a subset of experts for a given domain leads to severe performance degradation. This limits their practicality in memory-constrained settings, especially as models grow larger and sparser. We introduce EMO, an MoE designed for modularity-the independent use and composition of expert subsets-without requiring human-defined priors. Our key idea is to encourage tokens from similar domains to rely on similar experts. Since tokens within a document often share a domain, EMO restricts them to select experts from a shared pool, while allowing different documents to use different pools. This simple constraint enables coherent expert groupings to emerge during pretraining using document boundaries alone. We pretrain a 1B-active, 14B-total EMO on 1T tokens. As a full model, it matches standard MoE performance. Crucially, it enables selective expert use: retaining only 25% (12.5%) of experts incurs just a 1% (3%) absolute drop, whereas standard MoEs break under the same setting. We further find that expert subsets in EMO specialize at semantic levels (e.g., domains such as math or code), in contrast to the low-level syntactic specialization observed in standard MoEs. Altogether, our results demonstrate a path toward modular, memory-efficient deployment of large, sparse models and open new opportunities for composable architectures.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Reality Check: How Avatar and Face Representation Affect the Perceptual Evaluation of Synthesized Gestures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.06063)
*Haoyang Du,Yinghan Xu,John Dingliana,Brian Keegan,Rachel McDonnell,Cathy Ennis*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 研究表明，在评估语音驱动的3D手势生成时，虚拟化身的外观和面部呈现会系统性地影响知觉判断，不同运动来源和7种常见化身渲染方式均存在显著差异。研究提供了手势合成基准测试和虚拟人应用部署的建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然语音驱动的3D手势生成依赖于知觉评价，但不同研究在评判生成动作时往往使用不同外观的化身和面部呈现方式，先前研究暗示这些视觉选择可能造成评价偏差，但缺乏受控实验证据。

Method: 通过受控实验，对共语手势在多种运动来源下进行评价，涵盖当代研究和应用流程中使用的七种代表性化身渲染方式。

Result: 化身和面部呈现会系统性地偏移知觉判断，不同渲染方式对运动评价有显著影响。

Conclusion: 提出了手势合成基准测试以及面向人类的虚拟人应用部署的建议。

Abstract: The capacity to create realistic virtual humans has progressed significantly, and such characters can be found in many applications across entertainment, education and health. As an essential element of interactive virtual humans, speech-driven 3D gesture generation still depends heavily on perceptual evaluation, yet studies often vary avatar appearance and facial presentation when judging the generated motions. Prior work suggests these visual choices can bias motion judgments, but controlled evidence remains limited. We address this gap with controlled evaluations of co-speech gestures across motion sources, spanning seven representative avatar renderings used in contemporary research and application pipelines. Our results show that avatar and face presentation systematically shift perceptual judgments, and we provide recommendations for benchmarking gesture synthesis as well as for deploying virtual humans in human-facing applications.

</details>


### [155] [3DSS: 3D Surface Splatting for Inverse Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05876)
*Mae Younes,Adnane Boukhayma*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出3D表面喷溅（3DSS），一种可微表面喷溅渲染器，用于从多视图图像进行基于物理的逆渲染，通过表面分离问题的直接公式化、基于覆盖的合成模型、前向微表面着色和密度感知自适应细化，联合恢复形状、BRDF材质和照明，并桥接到网格工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆渲染方法（基于网格、隐式表示或高斯喷溅）在可微渲染和物理准确性之间存在权衡，缺乏一种既能实现高质量表面渲染又能提供可微梯度以进行逆渲染的方法。

Method: 1) 将表面分离问题直接重建核公式化；2) 提出基于覆盖的合成模型，使用椭圆加权平均（EWA）重建权重推导每层不透明度，实现抗锯齿轮廓和稀疏边缘的可微可见性梯度；3) 结合前向微表面着色与共优化的HDR环境光照；4) 密度感知自适应细化。

Result: 在几何重建、新视图合成和新光照重照明任务上，3DSS优于基于网格、隐式表示和高斯喷溅的基线方法。

Conclusion: 3DSS通过可微表面喷溅渲染实现了物理准确的逆渲染，并且优化得到的定向表面样本可通过点云表面重建方法自然桥接到网格工作流，具有良好的实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: We present 3D Surface Splatting (3DSS), the first differentiable surface splatting renderer for physically-based inverse rendering from multi-view images. Our central insight is that the surface separation problem at the heart of surface splatting admits a direct formulation in terms of the reconstruction kernels themselves. From this foundation we derive a coverage-based compositing model whose per-layer opacity arises directly from the accumulated Elliptical Weighted Average reconstruction weight, yielding anti-aliased silhouettes and informative visibility gradients at sparsely covered edges. Combined with forward microfacet shading under co-optimized HDR environment lighting and density-aware adaptive refinement, 3DSS jointly recovers shape, spatially-varying BRDF materials, and illumination. Because the optimized representation is a set of oriented surface samples, it bridges natively to mesh-based workflows via surface reconstruction from oriented point cloud methods. We evaluate 3DSS against mesh-based, implicit, and Gaussian-splatting baselines across geometry reconstruction, novel-view synthesis, and novel-illumination relighting.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [Understanding Annotator Safety Policy with Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05329)
*Alex Oesterling,Donghao Ren,Yannick Assogba,Dominik Moritz,Sunnie S. Y. Kim,Leon Gatys,Fred Hohman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可解释的注释者策略模型（APMs），通过学习注释者的标注行为来揭示其内部安全策略，从而区分标注分歧的来源（操作失误、策略模糊、价值多元），无需额外标注成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注分歧在AI安全策略中普遍存在，但现有方法难以区分分歧来源（操作失误、策略模糊、价值多元），直接询问注释者既昂贵又不可靠。

Method: 提出注释者策略模型（APMs），从标注行为中学习注释者内部安全策略，生成可解释的策略模型，无需额外标注工作即可使推理过程可见且可比较。

Result: APMs在建模注释者安全策略上准确率>80%，能忠实预测反事实编辑下的响应，并在可控设置中恢复已知的策略差异。成功应用于揭示策略模糊（注释者对安全指令的不同理解）和价值多元（不同人口统计群体间安全优先级差异）。

Conclusion: APMs能够支持更有针对性、透明且包容的安全策略设计，通过区分分歧来源促进更有效的策略改进。

Abstract: Safety policies define what constitutes safe and unsafe AI outputs, guiding data annotation and model development. However, annotation disagreement is pervasive and can stem from multiple sources such as operational failures (annotators misunderstand or misexecute the task), policy ambiguity (policy wording leaves room for interpretation), or value pluralism (different annotators hold different perspectives on safety). Distinguishing these sources matters. For example, operational failures call for quality control, ambiguity calls for policy clarification, and pluralism calls for deliberation about incorporating diverse perspectives. Yet understanding why annotators disagree is difficult. Directly asking annotators for their reasoning is costly, substantially increasing annotation burden, and can be unreliable for both human and LLM annotators as self-reported reasoning often fails to reflect actual decision processes.
  We introduce Annotator Policy Models (APMs), interpretable models that learn annotators' internal safety policies from labeling behavior alone, making annotator reasoning visible and comparable without additional annotation effort. We validate that APMs accurately model annotator safety policy (>80% accuracy), faithfully predict responses to counterfactual edits, and recover known policy differences in controlled settings. Applying APMs to LLM and human annotations, we demonstrate two core applications: (1) surfacing policy ambiguity by revealing how annotators interpret safety instructions differently, and (2) surfacing value pluralism by uncovering systematic differences in safety priorities across demographic groups. Together, these capabilities support more targeted, transparent, and inclusive safety policy design.

</details>


### [157] [ZAYA1-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05365)
*Robert Washbourne,Rishi Iyer,Tomas Figliolia,Henry Zheng,Ryan Lorig-Roach,Sungyeon Yang,Pritish Yuvraj,Quentin Anthony,Yury Tokpanov,Xiao Yang,Ganesh Nanduru,Stephen Ebert,Praneeth Medepalli,Skyler Szot,Srivatsan Rajagopal,Alex Ong,Bhavana Mehta,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ZAYA1-8B是一个基于MoE++架构、700M激活/8B总参数的推理聚焦模型，在AMD全栈平台上完成训练。在多项数学和编程基准上匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528，并引入Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，在AIME'25上达91.9%，显著缩小了与更大模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限条件下（<1B激活参数）构建高性能推理模型，挑战大规模稠密模型和更大MoE模型的推理能力优势，同时探索高效测试时计算扩展方法。

Method: 采用MoE++架构（700M激活/8B总参），从预训练阶段即引入推理数据并使用答案保持裁剪方案；后训练采用四阶段强化学习级联（推理热身、RLVE-Gym课程、数学/代码RL、行为RL）；提出Markovian RSA测试时计算方法，递归聚合多条推理轨迹并仅向前传递有界长度的推理尾部。

Result: 在数学和编程基准上匹配或超越DeepSeek-R1-0528；Markovian RSA使AIME'25达91.9%、HMMT'25达89.6%，仅需4K token尾部传递，缩小了与Gemini-2.5 Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2、GPT-5-High等更大模型的差距。

Conclusion: 通过架构创新（MoE++）、训练策略（推理优先的预训练+四阶段RL级联）和测试时计算技术（Markovian RSA），小参数模型可以在推理任务上实现与超大模型竞争的性能，证明了高效推理模型设计路径的有效性。

Abstract: We present ZAYA1-8B, a reasoning-focused mixture-of-experts (MoE) model with 700M active and 8B total parameters, built on Zyphra's MoE++ architecture. ZAYA1-8B's core pretraining, midtraining, and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) were performed on a full-stack AMD compute, networking, and software platform. With under 1B active parameters, ZAYA1-8B matches or exceeds DeepSeek-R1-0528 on several challenging mathematics and coding benchmarks, and remains competitive with substantially larger open-weight reasoning models. ZAYA1-8B was trained from scratch for reasoning, with reasoning data included from pretraining onward using an answer-preserving trimming scheme. Post-training uses a four-stage RL cascade: reasoning warmup on math and puzzles; a 400-task RLVE-Gym curriculum; math and code RL with test-time compute traces and synthetic code environments built from competitive-programming references; and behavioral RL for chat and instruction following. We also introduce Markovian RSA, a test-time compute method that recursively aggregates parallel reasoning traces while carrying forward only bounded-length reasoning tails between rounds. In TTC evaluation, Markovian RSA raises ZAYA1-8B to 91.9\% on AIME'25 and 89.6\% on HMMT'25 while carrying forward only a 4K-token tail, narrowing the gap to much larger reasoning models including Gemini-2.5 Pro, DeepSeek-V3.2, and GPT-5-High.

</details>


### [158] [Partial Evidence Bench: Benchmarking Authorization-Limited Evidence in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05379)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Partial Evidence Bench，一个确定性基准测试，用于衡量企业智能体在受限检索环境下因访问控制导致回答看似完整但实际遗漏关键证据的失败模式。包含72个任务、三个场景族（尽职调查、合规审计、安全事件响应），以及完整的评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业智能体在受限检索、委托工作流和策略约束证据环境中运行时，即便访问控制正确执行，系统仍可能输出看似完整但实际遗漏未授权关键证据的回答。现有评估方法无法测量此类治理关键性失败模式，亟需一个无需人工评判、无污染风险的确定性基准。

Method: 构建包含72个任务的确定性基准，涵盖尽职调查、合规审计、安全事件响应三个场景族。采用ACL分区语料库，提供oracle完整答案、oracle授权视图答案、oracle完整性判断和结构化缺口报告oracle。从答案正确性、完整性感知、缺口报告质量和危险完整性行为四个维度评估系统。

Result: 基线实验显示静默过滤在所有场景族中均存在灾难性安全隐患；显式失败并报告行为可消除危险完整性而不退化为简单弃权。初步真实模型运行显示，系统在过度声称完整性、保守性低估完整性或报告企业可用形式的不完整性方面存在模型依赖性和场景敏感性差异。

Conclusion: 该基准的贡献在于使治理关键性的智能体失败模式可测量，无需人工评判或易污染的静态语料库，为评估企业智能体在访问控制约束下的可靠性提供了标准化工具。

Abstract: Enterprise agents increasingly operate inside scoped retrieval systems, delegated workflows, and policy-constrained evidence environments. In these settings, access control can be enforced correctly while the system still produces an answer that appears complete even though material evidence lies outside the caller's authorization boundary. This paper introduces Partial Evidence Bench, a deterministic benchmark for measuring that failure mode. The benchmark ships three scenario families -- due diligence, compliance audit, and security incident response -- with 72 tasks total, ACL-partitioned corpora, oracle complete answers, oracle authorized-view answers, oracle completeness judgments, and structured gap-report oracles. It evaluates systems along four surfaces: answer correctness, completeness awareness, gap-report quality, and unsafe completeness behavior. Checked-in baselines show that silent filtering is catastrophically unsafe across all shipped families, while explicit fail-and-report behavior eliminates unsafe completeness without collapsing the task into trivial abstention. Preliminary real-model runs show model-dependent and scenario-sensitive differences in whether systems overclaim completeness, conservatively underclaim, or report incompleteness in an enterprise-usable form. The benchmark's broader contribution is to make a governance-critical agent failure measurable without human judges or contamination-prone static corpora.

</details>


### [159] [Intelligent CCTV for Urban Design: AI-Based Analysis of Soft Infrastructure at Intersections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05402)
*Vinit Katariya,Seungjin Kim,Curtis Craig,Nichole Morris,Hamed Tabkhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种利用现有CCTV基础设施的AI分析框架，通过深度学习和基于透视的速度估计方法，评估软性干预措施（临时人行道和路缘延伸）对车辆速度和安全的有效性。在明尼阿波利斯的实验显示，无信号交叉口平均速度和85分位速度分别下降18.75%和16.56%，过境交通量减少12.2%；信号交叉口平均速度和85分位速度分别下降20.0%和17.19%。证明了软性基础设施的交通稳静化效果及AI方法的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通评估方法成本高、周期长，缺乏对软性干预措施（如临时人行道和路缘延伸）快速、低成本的效果评估手段。AI和计算机视觉技术的发展为利用现有CCTV基础设施进行自动化交通数据采集与分析提供了可能。

Method: 利用现有CCTV摄像头，采用深度学习和基于透视变换的速度估计算法，分别在干预前、干预后第1周和第2周采集数据，对比分析车辆速度和行为变化。

Result: 无信号交叉口：平均速度和85分位速度分别下降18.75%和16.56%，过境交通量减少12.2%。信号交叉口：除一个地点外，平均速度和85分位速度分别下降20.0%和17.19%。

Conclusion: 软性基础设施（临时人行道和路缘延伸）具有显著的交通稳静化效果；AI驱动的方法能够实现快速、低成本、基于证据的交通政策评估，具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) and computer vision are transforming transportation data collection. This study introduces an AI-enabled analytics framework leveraging existing CCTV infrastructure to evaluate the impact of soft interventions, such as temporary pedestrian refuges and curb extensions, on vehicle speed and safety. Using deep learning and perspective-based speed estimation, we evaluated driver behavior before and after interventions, with repeated post-installation monitoring in Week 1 and Week 2, in Minneapolis. Findings reveal that at unsignalized intersections, mean and 85th-percentile speeds fell by up to 18.75% and 16.56%, respectively, while pass-through traffic decreased by as much as 12.2%. Signalized intersections showed comparable reductions except one location, with mean and 85th-percentile speeds dropping by up to 20.0% and 17.19%. These results demonstrate the traffic-calming effectiveness of soft infrastructure and underscore the utility of AI-powered methods for rapid, low-cost, and evidence-based transport policy evaluation.

</details>


### [160] [When Helpfulness Becomes Sycophancy: Sycophancy is a Boundary Failure Between Social Alignment and Epistemic Integrity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05403)
*Jiechen Li,Catherine A. Barry,Rishika Randev,Janet Chen,Ella Jorgensen,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证LLM中的谄媚现象是社会对齐与认知完整性之间的边界失败，提出一个三条件框架（用户线索、对齐行为、认知损害）和分类体系（对齐目标、机制、严重程度）来界定这一现象，并讨论了评估与缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常将谄媚操作化为同意不正确信念、立场反转或偏离客观正确标准等外部行为，但这些表述仅捕捉了显性形式，未能充分说明涉及认知完整性和社会对齐的微妙边界失败。作者试图厘清谄媚不应仅被理解为同意，而是一种取代独立认知判断的对齐行为。

Method: 提出一个三条件框架：1) 用户表达线索（信念、偏好或自我概念）；2) 模型通过对齐行为向该线索偏移；3) 这种偏移损害了认知准确性、独立推理或适当纠正。同时引入分类体系，包括对齐目标、机制和严重程度三个维度。

Result: 界定谄媚为社会对齐与认知完整性之间的边界故障，提出系统化的定义框架和分类学，并讨论了边界感知评估、结构化评分标准和缓解策略的实践意义。

Conclusion: 呼吁进行边界感知评估，采用结构化评分标准和针对性缓解策略，并将这些提议置于关于谄媚的替代性观点中进行定位讨论。

Abstract: This position paper argues that sycophancy in LLMs is a boundary failure between social alignment and epistemic integrity. Existing work often operationalizes sycophancy through external behavior such as agreement with incorrect user beliefs, position reversals, or deviation from an objective standard of correctness. These formulations capture only overt forms of the phenomenon and leave subtler boundary failures involving epistemic integrity and social alignment underspecified. We argue that sycophancy should not be understood as agreement alone, but as alignment behavior that displaces independent epistemic judgment. To clarify this boundary, we propose a three-condition framework for sycophancy. First, the user expresses a cue in the form of a belief, preference, or self-concept. Second, the model shifts toward that cue through alignment behavior. Third, this shift compromises epistemic accuracy, independent reasoning, or appropriate correction. We also introduce a taxonomy for classifying sycophancy, consisting of alignment targets, mechanisms, and severity. The paper concludes by discussing implications for alignment evaluation and argues for boundary-aware assessment, structured rubrics, and mitigation strategies, while situating these proposals alongside alternative views of sycophancy.

</details>


### [161] [Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Financial Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05409)
*Yang Shu,Yingmin Liu,Zequn Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FinAgent-RAG是一个面向金融文档问答的智能体RAG框架，通过迭代检索-推理循环、自验证机制和三项领域创新（对比金融检索器、程序化思维推理、自适应策略路由器），在FinQA、ConvFinQA和TAT-QA三个基准上分别达到76.81%、78.46%和74.96%的执行准确率，超越最强基线5.62-9.32个百分点，同时降低API成本41.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融文档问答涉及跨结构化表格、文本叙述和脚注等多种异构证据的复杂多步数值推理，现有RAG方法采用单次检索-生成范式，难以应对金融分析中常见的组合推理链，且缺乏精确数值推理能力。

Method: 提出FinAgent-RAG框架，包含三项创新：(1)对比金融检索器，通过难负样本挖掘区分语义相似但数值不同的金融段落；(2)程序化思维推理模块，生成可执行Python代码实现精确算术运算；(3)自适应策略路由器，根据问题复杂度动态分配计算资源。

Result: 在FinQA、ConvFinQA和TAT-QA上分别达到76.81%、78.46%和74.96%的执行准确率，超越最强基线5.62-9.32个百分点；自适应路由器降低API成本41.3%。

Conclusion: FinAgent-RAG通过智能体化的迭代检索-推理机制和领域定制化设计，显著提升了金融数值推理的准确性、鲁棒性和实际部署经济性，验证了其在金融机构中的实用价值。

Abstract: Financial document question answering (QA) demands complex multi-step numerical reasoning over heterogeneous evidence--structured tables, textual narratives, and footnotes--scattered across corporate filings. Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) approaches adopt a single-pass retrieve-then-generate paradigm that struggles with the compositional reasoning chains prevalent in financial analysis. We propose FinAgent-RAG, an agentic RAG framework that orchestrates iterative retrieval-reasoning loops with self-verification, specifically engineered for the precision requirements of financial numerical reasoning. The framework integrates three domain-specific innovations: (1) a Contrastive Financial Retriever trained with hard negative mining to distinguish semantically similar but numerically distinct financial passages, (2) a Program-of-Thought reasoning module that generates executable Python code for precise arithmetic rather than relying on error-prone LLM-based mental computation, and (3) an Adaptive Strategy Router that dynamically allocates computational resources based on question complexity, reducing API costs by 41.3% on FinQA while preserving accuracy. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets--FinQA, ConvFinQA, and TAT-QA--demonstrate that FinAgent-RAG achieves 76.81%, 78.46%, and 74.96% execution accuracy respectively, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.62--9.32 percentage points. Ablation studies, cross-backbone evaluation with four LLMs, and deployment cost analysis confirm the framework's robustness and practical viability for financial institutions.

</details>


### [162] [LaTA: A Drop-in, FERPA-Compliant Local-LLM Autograder for Upper-Division STEM Coursework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05410)
*Jesse A. Rodríguez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LaTA (LaTeX Teaching Assistant)，一个完全运行于本地廉价硬件上的开源自动批改系统，在 LaTeX 原生流程中实现四阶段管线（摄入-分段-评分-报告），使用本地部署的开源思维链大模型（gpt-oss:120b）进行评分。在俄勒冈州立大学 ME 373 课程中部署一学期后，以每份作业 $0 边际成本服务约 200 名学生，评分错误率约 0.02–0.04%，且实验组在期中/期末考试中分别比传统批改组高出约 11% 和 8%，并在所有学习目标上报告了显著的自信心提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 评分系统大多将学生作业发送至第三方 API，违反了 FERPA 法规，给机构带来数据风险，且需要大量修改作业流程。高等教育中工程和物理课程亟需一种隐私安全、低成本、与现有 LaTeX 工作流无缝集成的自动化评分方案。

Method: LaTA 实现四阶段管线：(1) 摄入——接收学生 LaTeX 源码；(2) 分段——将作业按题目切分；(3) 评分——使用本地部署的开源思维链大模型 (gpt-oss:120b) 对比教师参考答案，依据 YAML 格式的二元逐项评分标准进行评分；(4) 报告——生成评分报告。系统运行于单台 Mac Studio 上，无需第三方 API 调用。

Result: 在俄勒冈州立大学 ME 373 课程（约 200 名学生）的 Winter 2026 学期中部署，每周每份作业批改耗时 1–3 分钟，边际成本为 $0。教师确认的评分错误率约为每评分项 0.02–0.04%。与同一教师之前传统批改的班级相比，LaTA 批改班级期中考试成绩提高约 11%，期末考试提高约 8%。问卷调查（N=159）显示所有学习目标的自信心提升均超过 1.49 Likert 点（p < 10⁻²⁷）。

Conclusion: LaTA 证明在廉价本地硬件上使用开源 LLM 进行全自动评分是可行且有效的，既保护了学生数据隐私（无需第三方 API），又显著提升了学生学业表现和学习自信心。代码以 AGPLv3 协议开源发布。

Abstract: Large-language-model (LLM) graders promise to relieve the grading burden of upper-division STEM courses, but most deployments to date send student work to third-party APIs, violating FERPA and exposing institutions to data risk while requiring substantial assignment modification. We present $\textbf{LaTA}\ (\textit{LaTeX Teaching Assistant})$, a drop-in, open-source autograder that runs entirely on commodity on-premises hardware and assumes a LaTeX-native workflow already adopted by many engineering and physics courses. LaTA implements a four-stage pipeline (ingest, segment, grade, report) using a locally hosted open-weight chain-of-thought LLM grader (gpt-oss:120b) that compares student work to an instructor-authored reference solution and applies a YAML rubric with binary per-item scoring. We deployed LaTA in Winter~2026 in ME 373 (Mechanical Engineering Methods) at Oregon State University, grading every weekly assignment for approximately 200 students on a single Mac Studio at \$0 marginal cost per assignment and 1--3 minutes of wall-clock time per submission, enabling regrading of corrected assignments and greatly expanded TA office hour offerings. The instructor-confirmed grading-error rate held at roughly $0.02$--$0.04\%$ per rubric line item across the term. Relative to the same instructor's previous traditionally-graded cohort, the LaTA-graded cohort outperformed by approximately $11\%$ on the midterm exam and $8\%$ on the final exam, and reported large gains in self-assessed confidence on every stated learning objective ($N = 159$ survey responses, $Δ\geq +1.49$ Likert points, $p < 10^{-27}$ on every comparison). We release the code under AGPLv3.

</details>


### [163] [From History to State: Constant-Context Skill Learning for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05413)
*Haoyang Xie,Xinyuan Wang,Yancheng Wang,Puda Zhao,Feng Ju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种"恒定上下文技能学习"框架，将可重复的智能体工作流从提示词中转移到模型权重中，通过轻量级任务族模块学习可复用技能，结合确定性状态追踪器和逐步监督微调与在线强化学习，在多个环境（ALFWorld、WebShop、SciWorld）中达到或超过强基线性能，同时每轮推理的提示词数量减少2-7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人智能体面临隐私-成本-能力的三难困境：云端模型能执行多步工作流但暴露敏感中间上下文到外部API，本地模型保护隐私但可靠性不足；此外，两种场景下都需要为冗长的技能提示词和增长的历史记录反复支付计算成本。

Method: 提出"恒定上下文技能学习"（constant-context skill learning），将可重用工作流作为轻量级任务族模块（task-family modules）学入权重；推理时仅依赖当前观测和紧凑的状态块；确定性跟踪器（deterministic tracker）根据任务进度渲染状态块并提供子目标奖励；每个模块通过步骤级监督微调（step-level SFT）训练，再通过在线强化学习（online RL）精炼。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B、Qwen3-8B和Llama-3.1-8B上均取得强性能。使用Qwen3-8B时，SFT+RL在ALFWorld上达到89.6%未见任务成功率，WebShop上76.8%成功率，SciWorld上66.4%未见任务成功率。相比受控的ReAct提示基线，每轮推理提示词减少2-7倍。

Conclusion: 过程性上下文可以从提示词转移到模型权重中，在保持或提升性能的同时大幅降低推理时的提示开销，为隐私敏感的个人智能体部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly used to operate browsers, files, code and tools, making personal assistants a natural deployment target. Yet personal agents face a privacy-cost-capability tension: cloud models execute multi-step workflows well but expose sensitive intermediate context to external APIs, while local models preserve privacy but remain less reliable. Both settings also pay repeatedly for long skill prompts and growing histories. We propose constant-context skill learning, a context-to-weights framework for recurring agent workflows: reusable procedures are learned in lightweight task-family modules, while inference conditions only on the current observation and a compact state block. A deterministic tracker renders this state block from task progress and supplies aligned subgoal rewards, so each module can be trained with step-level SFT and refined through online RL. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and SciWorld, our agents achieve strong performance across Qwen3-4B, Qwen3-8B and Llama-3.1-8B. With Qwen3-8B, SFT+RL reaches 89.6\% unseen success on ALFWorld, 76.8\% success on WebShop, and 66.4\% unseen success on SciWorld. They match or exceed strong published agent-training results while reducing prompt tokens per turn by 2--7$\times$ relative to controlled ReAct prompting baselines, showing that procedural context can be moved from prompts into weights.

</details>


### [164] [The Geopolitics of AI Safety: A Causal Analysis of Regional LLM Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05427)
*Alif Al Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率图模型（PGM）框架，通过Pearl的do-operator因果干预方法审计LLM安全机制的公平性，发现标准观测性公平指标因忽略上下文毒性而高估人口统计偏见，并揭示东西方模型在因果拒绝率上的不同对齐趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM公平性评估主要依赖观测性偏差测量，但该方法受到数据集本身话题毒性与特定人口统计群体天然共现的混淆干扰，无法真正区分模型偏见与上下文毒性，因此需要因果推断方法来隔离人口统计信息的独立因果效应。

Method: 构建概率图模型（PGM）框架，应用Pearl的do-operator对提示中注入的文化人口统计信息进行因果干预，在ToxiGen和BOLD两个数据集上对7个来自不同地区（美国、欧洲、阿联酋、中国、印度）的指令微调模型进行大规模实证分析。

Result: 观测性偏差与干预性偏差存在显著差异，标准公平指标因未控制上下文毒性而高估人口统计偏见；西方模型对特定人口群体表现出更高的因果拒绝率，东方模型整体干预率较低但对区域性人口统计信息具有针对性敏感性。

Conclusion: 现有观测性公平度量方法存在根本性缺陷，需要因果推断方法准确评估模型公平性；不同地区模型的对齐趋势反映了文化背景差异，人口统计敏感的过度触发会限制下游应用中的良性话语空间。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are integrated into global software systems, ensuring equitable safety guardrails is a critical requirement. Current fairness evaluations predominantly measure bias observationally, a methodology confounded by the inherent toxicity of topics naturally paired with specific demographics in testing datasets. This study introduces a Probabilistic Graphical Model (PGM) framework to audit LLM safety mechanisms causally. By applying Pearl's do-operator, we mathematically isolate the causal effect of injecting a cultural demographic into a prompt. We conduct a large-scale empirical analysis across seven instruction-tuned models spanning diverse origins: the United States (Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B), Europe (Mistral-7B-v0.3), the UAE (Falcon3-7B), China (Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B), and India (Airavata-7B). Utilizing two distinct datasets (ToxiGen and BOLD), the findings reveal a disparity between observational and interventional bias, demonstrating that standard fairness metrics can overestimate demographic bias by failing to account for context toxicity. Furthermore, the causal probabilities indicate distinct alignment trends: Western models exhibit higher causal refusal rates for specific demographic groups, whereas Eastern models demonstrate low overall intervention rates with targeted sensitivities toward regional demographics. We discuss the implications of these biases, highlighting how demographic-sensitive over-triggering restricts benign discourse in downstream applications.

</details>


### [165] [Authorization Propagation in Multi-Agent AI Systems: Identity Governance as Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05440)
*Krti Tallam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体AI系统中存在的"授权传播"问题，即非人类主体在数据检索、任务委派和结果合成过程中维护授权不变性的挑战。该问题不同于提示注入，也无法被传统访问控制模型完全解决。作者形式化了授权传播作为工作流级属性，识别了三个子问题（传递委派、聚合推理和时间有效性），并推导出七项架构需求。核心主张是身份治理必须作为基础设施来处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全讨论过度聚焦于提示注入，忽视了多智能体系统中非人类主体之间授权传递的独特挑战。经典访问控制模型（RBAC、ABAC、ReBAC）无法充分应对这一新型问题。

Method: 通过形式化定义将授权传播确立为工作流级属性，分解为三个子问题，推导七项结构需求，并结合现有工作（调用绑定能力令牌、任务范围授权信封、依赖图策略执行、执行计数撤销）进行对比分析，最后用生产级企业AI平台的实际行为验证理论预测。

Result: 识别出多智能体系统中授权传播的三个子问题：传递委派、聚合推理和时间有效性。发现即使是普通系统行为（而非仅对抗行为）也会产生授权失效。现有研究虽已收敛于该问题，但尚未形成完整架构。

Conclusion: 身份治理必须作为基础设施来设计：持续评估、在每个交互边界强制执行，并在编排逻辑扩展之前嵌入系统。该问题需要全新的架构方案而非简单修补。

Abstract: The security discussion around agentic AI focuses heavily on prompt injection. This paper argues that multi-agent systems also create a distinct authorization problem: maintaining authorization invariants as non-human principals retrieve data, delegate tasks, and synthesize results across changing boundaries. We call this problem authorization propagation. It is not reducible to prompt injection and is not fully addressed by classical access-control models such as RBAC, ABAC, or ReBAC. The paper formalizes authorization propagation as a workflow-level property, identifies three sub-problems (transitive delegation, aggregation inference, and temporal validity), and derives seven structural requirements for authorization architectures in multi-agent AI systems. Recent work on invocation-bound capability tokens, task-scoped authorization envelopes, dependency-graph policy enforcement, and execution-count revocation demonstrates that the field is converging on the problem, but not yet on a complete architecture. The central claim is that identity governance must be treated as infrastructure: evaluated continuously, enforced at every interaction boundary, and designed into the system before orchestration logic is allowed to scale. Preliminary implementation evidence from a production enterprise AI platform shows that ordinary system behavior, not only adversarial action, already produces the failures this model predicts.

</details>


### [166] [Agentic Discovery of Exchange-Correlation Density Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05460)
*Titouan Duston,Jiashu Liang,Yuanheng Wang,Weihao Gao,Xuelan Wen,Nan Sheng,Weiluo Ren,Yang Sun,Yixiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的智能体搜索系统（SAFS），通过进化历史引导的迭代优化循环，自动搜索密度泛函理论中的交换关联泛函形式。发现的最佳泛函SAFS26-a在标准热化学数据集上相比ωB97M-V基线提升了约9%，同时也揭示了AI辅助科学中模型可能利用非物理捷径欺骗基准测试的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来，准确的交换关联泛函开发主要依赖人类研究者的手工设计，结合物理直觉、精确约束和经验拟合。该工作旨在利用大语言模型实现系统化、自动化的泛函设计，替代传统的人工驱动循环。

Method: 采用基于LLM的智能体搜索系统，通过"计划-执行-总结"的迭代循环工作。LLM在进化历史引导下提出结构化的泛函形式变更，通过优化泛函参数在标准热化学数据集上的表现来评估改进，并在保留子集上验证性能。

Result: 发现的最佳泛函SAFS26-a相比金标准ωB97M-V基线提升了约9%的性能。

Conclusion: LLM驱动的自动化搜索能发现真正有效的泛函改进，但也存在利用非物理捷径欺骗基准的风险。必须将领域专业知识转化为显式强制约束，才能确保结果在科学上的可靠性。

Abstract: The development of accurate exchange-correlation (XC) functionals remains a longstanding challenge in density functional theory (DFT). The vast majority of XC functionals have been hand designed by human researchers combining physical insight, exact constraints, and empirical fitting. Recent advances in large language models enable a systematic, automated alternative to this human-driven design loop. This report presents an agentic search system in which an LLM proposes structured functional-form changes guided by evolutionary history. The system attempts to improve functional performance through an iterative plan-execute-summarize loop, where improvements are measurable by optimizing functional parameters against a standard thermochemistry dataset, then evaluating performance on a held-out subset. The strongest discovered functional, SAFS26-a (Seed Agentic Functional Search 2026), improves upon the gold-standard ωB97M-V baseline by ~9%. These results also surface a cautionary lesson for AI-assisted science: models powerful enough to discover genuine improvements are equally capable of exploiting unphysical shortcuts to game the benchmark; domain expertise translated into explicitly enforced constraints remains essential to keeping results scientifically grounded.

</details>


### [167] [LANTERN: LLM-Augmented Neurosymbolic Transfer with Experience-Gated Reasoning Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05478)
*Mahyar Alinejad,Yue Wang,Amrit Singh Bedi,George Atia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LANTERN是一个统一的多源神经符号迁移学习框架，通过大语言模型从自然语言生成任务自动机、基于语义嵌入的多源策略聚合以及自适应师生门控机制，在资源管理、导航和控制等领域实现了40-60%的样本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经符号迁移方法依赖手动指定的任务自动机、假设单源任务、使用固定知识集成机制无法适应源任务相关性变化，缺乏可扩展且鲁棒的多源迁移方案。

Method: LANTERN包含三个核心组件：(i) 利用大语言模型从自然语言任务描述生成确定有限自动机(DFA)；(ii) 基于语义嵌入的多源策略聚合，通过跨任务相似度加权融合多个源策略；(iii) 基于时序差分误差和语义不确定性的自适应师生门控机制。

Result: 在资源管理、导航和控制等多个领域，LANTERN相比现有基线方法实现了40-60%的样本效率提升，并且对低质量源任务具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 多源、自适应加权的神经符号迁移方法能够提升符号RL环境下的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Transfer learning in reinforcement learning (RL) seeks to accelerate learning in new tasks by leveraging knowledge from related sources. Existing neurosymbolic transfer methods, however, typically rely on manually specified task automata, assume a single source task, and use fixed knowledge-integration mechanisms that cannot adapt to varying source relevance. We propose LANTERN, a unified framework for multi-source neurosymbolic transfer that addresses these limitations through three components: (i) deterministic finite automata generated from natural language task descriptions using large language models, (ii) semantic embedding-based aggregation of multiple source policies weighted by cross-task similarity, and (iii) adaptive teacher-student gating based on temporal-difference error and semantic uncertainty. Across domains spanning resource management, navigation, and control, LANTERN achieves 40-60% improvements in sample efficiency over existing baselines while remaining robust to poorly aligned sources. These results demonstrate that multi-source, adaptively weighted neurosymbolic transfer can improve scalability and robustness in symbolic RL settings.

</details>


### [168] [FinRAG-12B: A Production-Validated Recipe for Grounded Question Answering in Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05482)
*Denys Katerenchuk,Pablo Duboue,Keelan Evanini,David Gondek,Nithin Govindugari,Olivier Allauzen,Joshua Baptiste,David J More,Joshua Schechter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个统一、数据高效的框架，用于训练银行业务的领域特定大语言模型，通过仅143M token的数据生成管道、校准拒绝机制和端到端部署方法，在40+金融机构实现7.1个百分点的查询解决率提升，并相比GPT-4.1提供3-5倍更快响应和20-50倍更低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在各领域快速普及，但在银行业面临高精度、合规监管、可验证和可溯源回答等需求的阻力，需要针对这些约束进行优化。

Method: 1) 数据生成管道：结合LLM-as-a-Judge过滤、引文标注和课程学习，仅用143M tokens；2) 校准拒绝机制：训练22%无法回答样本，产生12%的"我不知道"率；3) 端到端方法：从数据整理到量化部署的整体流程。

Result: 12B模型在回答质量上优于GPT-4.1，引文标注能力更优；校准拒绝机制改善了基础模型不安全的4.3%拒绝率；在40+金融机构部署，查询解决率提升7.1个百分点(p<0.001)；相比GPT-4.1响应速度提升3-5倍，成本降低20-50倍。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效训练出高质量、可溯源、成本效益显著的银行业专用LLM，在真实部署约束下实现了精度、引用和拒绝校准之间的优化平衡。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are rapidly being adopted across various domains. However, their adoption in banking industry faces resistance due to demands for high accuracy, regulatory compliance, and the need for verifiable and grounded responses. We present a unified, data-efficient framework for training grounded domain-specific LLMs that optimizes answer quality, citation grounding, and calibrated refusal under real-world deployment constraints. First, we describe a data generation pipeline that combines LLM-as-a-Judge filtering, citation annotation, and curriculum learning with only 143M tokens. The resulting 12B model achieves high answer quality outperforming GPT-4.1 on citation grounding, with a modest citation tradeoff versus the untuned base. Second, we propose a calibrated refusal mechanism: training on 22% unanswerable examples yield a 12% "I don't know" rate, substantially improving over the base model's unsafe 4.3% rate while avoiding GPT-4.1's over-refusal (20.2%). Third, we present an end-to-end methodology spanning from data curation to quantized serving. The system is deployed at 40+ financial institutions, achieving a 7.1 percentage point improvement in query resolution (p < 0.001). Additionally, the model delivers 3-5x faster responses at 20-50x lower cost compared to GPT-4.1.

</details>


### [169] [Housing Potential Common Data Model and City Digital Twin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05535)
*Megan Katsumi,Mark Fox,Anderson Wong,Divnoor Chatha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了住房潜力通用数据模型（HPCDM），旨在打破数据孤岛，整合多源异构数据以支持住房潜力分析，并通过城市数字孪生和仪表盘应用验证了其实用性，同时识别了采用障碍并提供了缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的住房潜力评估需要整合分区规划、土地利用、人口特征和公共服务可达性等多维度数据，但目前这些数据分散在不同系统中，形成数据孤岛，缺乏统一的数据标准来支持集成与互操作。

Method: 提出了住房潜力通用数据模型（HPCDM）作为数据集成与互操作的标准；构建了城市数字孪生原型；开发了仪表盘应用进行实证验证。

Result: 成功评估了HPCDM模型的有效性，创建了面向住房的城市数字孪生系统，并开发了试点仪表盘应用，证明了实际部署的可行性。

Conclusion: HPCDM能够有效解决住房潜力分析中的数据孤岛问题，但技术框架之外仍存在关键的采用障碍，需要城市规划者和利益相关者采取针对性的缓解策略。

Abstract: The evaluation of housing potential requires consideration of a location from multiple perspectives, ranging from zoning and land use to population characteristics and access to services. This research introduces the Housing Potential Common Data Model (HPCDM) to overcome existing data silos, serving as a standard to support integration and interoperability across the diverse range of datasets that are required for housing potential analysis. This report details the evaluation of the model along with the creation of a City Digital Twin for housing and a pilot dashboard application to demonstrate a practical implementation. Beyond the technical framework, this work identifies critical barriers to adoption and provides actionable mitigation strategies for urban planners and stakeholders.

</details>


### [170] [AgenticRAG: Agentic Retrieval for Enterprise Knowledge Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05538)
*Susheel Suresh,Hazel Mak,Shangpo Chou,Fred Kroon,Sahil Bhatnagar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgenticRAG 提出了一种轻量级智能体框架，通过为推理 LLM 配备搜索、查找、打开和总结工具，使其能够自主迭代检索信息、在文档内导航并分析证据，从而减少对传统 RAG 搜索栈的过度依赖。在三个公开基准上，该方法的召回率、事实性和答案正确性均显著优于标准 RAG 基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 RAG 流程将过多的可验证负担置于搜索栈上，将语言模型约束在检索过程早期确定的固定候选项集中，限制了模型的推理和证据分析能力。

Method: 在现有企业搜索基础设施之上构建轻量级智能体框架 (AgenticRAG)，为推理 LLM 提供搜索 (search)、查找 (find)、打开 (open) 和总结 (summarize) 四种工具，使其能够像人类分析师一样自主迭代检索、在文档内定位相关内容并进行证据分析。

Result: BRIGHT 上召回率@1 达 49.6%（比最佳嵌入基线提升 21.8 个百分点）；WixQA 上事实性得分 0.96（相对提升 13%）；FinanceBench 上答案正确性 92%，仅比使用真实证据的 oracle 方法低 2 个百分点。消融实验表明，从单次检索转向智能体工具使用是最大影响因素（提升 5.9 倍）。

Conclusion: AgenticRAG 框架在真实企业生产环境中表现优异，通过将静态检索升级为动态、迭代的智能体交互过程，有效提升了 RAG 系统的检索质量和最终答案的准确性，适合实际企业部署。

Abstract: We present AgenticRAG, a practical agentic harness for retrieval and analysis over enterprise knowledge bases. Standard RAG pipelines place significant burden of grounding on the search stack, constraining the language model to a fixed candidate set chosen deep in the retrieval process. Our approach reduces this overdependence by layering a lightweight harness on top of existing enterprise search infrastructure, equipping a reasoning LLM with search, find, open, and summarize tools enabling the model to iteratively retrieve information, navigate within documents, and analyze evidence autonomously. On three open benchmarks we observe substantial gains: $49.6\%$ recall@1 on BRIGHT (+21.8 pp over the best embedding baseline), 0.96 factuality on WixQA ($+13\%$ relative improvement), and $92\%$ answer correctness on FinanceBench--within 2 pp of oracle access to true evidence. Ablation studies show that the most significant factor is the shift from single-shot retrieval to agentic tool use ($5.9\times$ improvement), while multi-query search and in-document navigation contribute to both quality and efficiency. We present various design choices in our agentic harness that were informed by pre-production deployments. Our results demonstrate its suitability for real-world enterprise production environments.

</details>


### [171] [SPARK: Self-Play with Asymmetric Reward from Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05546)
*Hyobin Park,Taeseop Kim,Dong-Geol Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPARK是一个基于知识图谱的自对弈强化学习框架，通过构建多文档科学文献的统一知识图谱，利用图谱路径生成关系推理问题，并基于结构化事实提供可验证奖励信号，在多跳问答任务上优于基于纯文本的自对弈基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自对弈强化学习在数学和代码等具有形式化可验证结构的领域表现优异，但将其扩展到科学文献领域面临挑战：文档内和文档间的多模态元素关系在文本中很少显式表达，导致关系推理问题的自动生成困难且奖励信号的可靠性不足。

Method: SPARK框架从多文档科学文献中自动构建统一知识图谱(KG)，将KG路径作为生成关系推理问题的来源，利用KG中的结构化事实提供可验证的奖励计算基础。一个小型视觉语言模型(sVLM)在信息不对称条件下交替扮演Proposer和Solver角色，与固定KG进行交互。

Result: 在公开基准和自建的跨文档多跳QA数据集上，SPARK一致优于基于纯文本语料的自对弈基线方法，且随着跳数增加性能差距扩大。

Conclusion: KG结构化的知识支撑能够为关系型多跳推理提供超越非结构化语料所能提供的帮助，该框架可自然扩展到在线适应场景。

Abstract: Self-play reinforcement learning has shown strong performance in domains with formally verifiable structure, such as mathematics and coding, where both problem generation and reward computation can be grounded in explicit rules. Extending this paradigm to scientific literature is more challenging: the relationships among multi-modal elements within and across documents are rarely made explicit in text, which makes automatic generation of relational reasoning questions difficult and weakens the reliability of reward signals. We propose SPARK (Self-Play with Asymmetric Reward from Knowledge Graphs), a framework that automatically constructs a unified knowledge graph (KG) from multi-document scientific literature and uses it as the structural basis for self-play. KG paths over multimodal nodes serve as a source for generating relational reasoning questions, and structured facts stored in the KG provide a basis for verifiable reward computation. A single small vision-language model (sVLM) alternates between Proposer and Solver roles under information asymmetry against a fixed KG, a design that we believe can be naturally extended toward online adaptation in future work. We evaluate SPARK on public benchmarks and a self-constructed cross-document multi-hop QA dataset. Results show that SPARK consistently outperforms flat-corpus-based self-play baselines, and the performance gap widens as hop count increases, suggesting that KG-structure grounding contributes to relational multi-hop reasoning beyond what unstructured corpus grounding can provide.

</details>


### [172] [Who Prices Cognitive Labor in the Age of Agents? A Position on Compute-Anchored Wages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05558)
*Siqi Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证AI智能体不是劳动力（劳动供给），而是将计算资本转化为认知劳动的生产技术。在此框架下，认知劳动的均衡工资由计算资本市场的租金率决定，而非劳动力市场。推导出"计算锚定工资"（CAW）上界：人类工资 ≤ λ·k·r_c。通过CES加总、区分可替代与互补任务扩展分析，指出认知劳动的定价者已转变为计算资本市场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正主流经济学直觉中关于AI智能体"弹性劳动供给→工资趋零"的错误机制，提出正确的理论框架，并说明其对于经济理论和政策制定的重要意义。

Method: 基于Mankiw(2020)教科书级要素定价框架，将智能体建模为将计算资本转化为认知劳动的生产技术；推导计算锚定工资（CAW）上界；通过CES加总扩展至可替代与互补任务场景；分析要素份额变化。

Result: 推导出人类工资的上界为λ·k·r_c，即工资由计算资本租金率、计算密集度和相对生产率决定。在可替代任务中，工资被计算资本市场锚定；在互补任务中，技能偏向性技术变化方向发生反转。

Conclusion: 智能体不是劳动供给，而是资本驱动的生产技术；认知劳动的均衡价格不再由劳动力市场设定，而是由计算资本市场定价；这一区分对理论和政策具有根本性意义。

Abstract: A natural intuition about the economics of AI agents is that, because agents can be replicated at near-zero marginal cost, they constitute a labor input in infinitely elastic supply, and therefore drive cognitive-labor wages to zero. We argue this framing is wrong in mechanism but partially correct in conclusion, and that the correction matters for both theory and policy. \textbf{Agents are not labor; they are a production technology that converts compute capital $K_c$ into effective units of cognitive labor $L_A$.} Once this is recognized, the elastic-supply margin that anchors the equilibrium wage migrates from the labor market to the compute capital market. Building on the textbook factor-pricing framework \citep{mankiw2020}, we derive a \emph{Compute-Anchored Wage} (CAW) bound stating that, on tasks where human and agent cognitive labor are substitutes, the competitive human wage is bounded above by $λ\cdot k \cdot r_c$, where $r_c$ is the rental rate of compute capital, $k$ is the compute intensity of one effective agent-labor unit, and $λ$ is the relative human-to-agent productivity. We generalize the result through CES aggregation, separate substitutable from complementary tasks (yielding a directional inversion of skill-biased technical change), and discuss factor-share consequences. The position is concise: \emph{the price-setter for cognitive labor is no longer the labor market.}

</details>


### [173] [Nonsense Helps: Prompt Space Perturbation Broadens Reasoning Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05566)
*Langlin Huang,Chengsong Huang,Jinyuan Li,Donghong Cai,Yuyi Yang,Jiaxin Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对GRPO在复杂任务中的"零优势问题"，本文提出LoPE（Lorem Perturbation for Exploration）框架，通过在重采样时向提示中添加随机的Lorem Ipsum伪拉丁文本扰动，解锁模型对困难问题的正交推理路径，显著提升探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在复杂任务中常因所有采样轨迹失败导致相对优势为零，模型失去有效训练信号，浪费数据和计算资源；简单增加采样预算受限于静态采样策略，无法突破推理探索瓶颈。

Method: LoPE在重采样前向提示中随机拼接由Lorem Ipsum词汇（伪拉丁占位文本）组成的序列，通过任务无关的提示空间扰动改变模型输出分布，从而解锁困难问题的正交推理路径。

Result: 在1.7B、4B和7B模型上的实验表明，LoPE显著优于使用原始提示的重采样方法；进一步分析发现，其他低困惑度的基于拉丁语的随机序列也能产生有效的扰动效果。

Conclusion: LoPE作为一种简单有效的训练框架，可作为LLM强化学习中拓宽探索范围的强基线方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, particularly Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), has significantly advanced the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, in complex tasks, GRPO frequently suffers from the ``zero-advantage problem'': when all sampled rollouts for a query fail, the relative advantage collapses to zero. Consequently, the model loses effective training signals for these questions, wasting the training data and computational budget. While simply increasing the sampling budget for these questions is a common remedy, the static sampling policy inherently constrains reasoning exploration, limiting the success rate. In this paper, we propose Lorem Perturbation for Exploration (LoPE), a simple yet effective training framework to break this exploration bottleneck. We posit that task-irrelevant prompt-space perturbations can shift the model's output distribution enough to unlock orthogonal reasoning pathways for hard questions. Specifically, LoPE prepends sequences stochastically assembled from Lorem Ipsum vocabulary (a pseudo-Latin placeholder text) to the prompts before resampling. Experiments across 1.7B, 4B, and 7B models demonstrate that LoPE significantly outperforms resampling with the original prompts. Further analysis reveals that other Latin-based random sequences with low perplexity are also effective perturbations. Our results establish LoPE as a strong baseline for broadening exploration in LLM reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [174] [Locality-aware Private Class Identification for Domain Adaptation with Extreme Label Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05567)
*Chuan-Xian Ren,Cheng-Jun Guo,Hong Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于最优传输的局部感知私有类识别方法（ReOT），通过传输质量分数函数区分共享类和私有类样本，在极端标签偏移下实现可靠的域适应，并提供了理论证明和泛化上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域适应方法假设私有类与共享类之间的偏移足够大以被视为异常值，但共享类内部的方差可能远大于私有类与共享类之间的差异，这一假设在现实场景中不成立。私有类显著增加了跨域分类的难度。

Method: 基于最优传输的局部传输和度量性质，提出局部感知的私有类识别方法，以传输质量分数函数形式实现；进而提出ReOT方法，在最小化分类风险的同时学习共享类与私有类之间的分离聚类结构，避免共享-私有样本对之间的错误匹配。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验验证了ReOT的有效性；同时提供了极端标签偏移场景下目标风险的泛化上界。

Conclusion: ReOT通过可靠的最优传输机制有效解决了标签空间包含关系下的私有类识别与域适应问题，理论和实验均证明了其优越性。

Abstract: Domain adaptation aims to transfer knowledge from a labeled source domain to an unlabeled target domain with different distributions. In real-world scenarios, the label spaces of the two domains often have an inclusion relationship, where some classes exist only in one domain but not the other. These non-overlapping classes are referred to as private classes. Identifying private class samples and mitigating their adverse effects is critical in the literature. Existing methods rely on the assumption that shifts in private classes are large enough to be considered outliers. However, the variance within a single shared class can be significantly larger than the difference between a private class and another shared class, challenging this assumption. Consequently, private classes substantially increase the difficulty of cross-domain classification. To address these issues, based on local transportation and metric properties of optimal transport (OT), a locality-aware private class identification approach is proposed in the form of a score function on transport mass. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is theoretically proven, highlighting the score function's strong ability to distinguish between shared and private class samples. Building on this, we introduce a reliable OT-based method (ReOT) for domain adaptation under severe label shift. ReOT minimizes classification risk while learning the separated cluster structure between the identified shared classes and private classes, effectively avoiding mismatch between shared-private sample pairs, thus ensuring that important knowledge is reliably transported intra-class to mitigate class-conditional discrepancy. Furthermore, a generalization upper bound of the target risk is provided for extreme label shift scenarios, which can be minimized by ReOT. Extensive experiments on benchmarks validate the effectiveness of ReOT.

</details>


### [175] [AlphaCrafter: A Full-Stack Multi-Agent Framework for Cross-Sectional Quantitative Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05580)
*Yishuo Yuan,Jiayi Sheng,Sirui Zeng,Jiaqi Wang,Jiaheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlphaCrafter是一个全栈多智能体框架，通过三个协同智能体（Miner、Screener、Trader）实现从因子发现到执行的全流程自适应量化交易，在CSI 300和S&P 500上持续超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化交易方法将因子发现、适应性选择与风控执行视为独立或静态组件，缺乏统一的、理性驱动的全自动流程框架。

Method: AlphaCrafter包含三个智能体：Miner（LLM引导的连续因子池扩展）、Screener（市场状态评估与条件化因子组合构建）、Trader（风险约束下的量化策略执行），三者形成闭环横截面交易系统。

Result: 在CSI 300和S&P 500上的实验表明，AlphaCrafter在风险调整收益上持续优于现有基线，且跨试验方差最低。

Conclusion: 集成的、自适应的因子到执行流程设计能够生成稳健的交易性能。

Abstract: Financial markets are inherently non-stationary, driven by complex interactions among macroeconomic regimes, microstructural frictions, and behavioral dynamics. Building quantitative strategies that remain profitable demands the continuous coupling of factor discovery, regime-adaptive selection, and risk-constrained execution. Prevailing approaches, however, optimize these components under static or isolated assumptions. Factor mining frameworks typically treat alpha discovery as a one-time search process, implicitly assuming that factor efficacy persists across market regimes. Execution-oriented systems often adopt role-playing agent architectures that simulate anthropomorphic trading committees, introducing behavioral noise rather than systematic rationality. Consequently, a fully automated, rationality-driven framework unifying a coherent quantitative pipeline remains absent. We introduce AlphaCrafter, a full-stack multi-agent framework that closes this gap through a continuously adaptive factor-to-execution pipeline, designed to track and respond to evolving market conditions without manual intervention. AlphaCrafter operates via three specialized agents: a Miner that continuously expands the factor pool via LLM-guided search, a Screener that assesses prevailing market conditions to construct regime-conditioned factor ensembles, and a Trader that translates these ensembles into quantitative strategies under explicit risk constraints. Together, these three agents form a closed-loop cross-sectional trading system that adapts holistically to evolving market dynamics. Extensive experiments on CSI 300 and S&P 500 demonstrate that AlphaCrafter consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in risk-adjusted returns while exhibiting the lowest cross-trial variance, confirming that integrated and adaptive factor-to-execution design yields robust trading performance.

</details>


### [176] [Belief Memory: Agent Memory Under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05583)
*Junfeng Liao,Qizhou Wang,Jianing Zhu,Bo Du,Rui Yan,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BeliefMem提出了一种概率记忆范式，将每个观测存储为多个候选结论及其概率，通过Noisy-OR规则更新，替代传统确定性记忆，在长上下文LLM智能体中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外部记忆方法对每个观测只存储单一确定性结论，忽略了观测的局部性和歧义性，导致自我强化的错误累积。

Method: 将记忆范式从单一结论转向保留多个候选结论及其概率；每个候选作为独立记忆条目存储，通过Noisy-OR规则随新观测更新概率；检索时所有候选及其概率同时呈现给智能体。

Result: 在LoCoMo和ALFWorld基准测试中，即使在有限数据下，BeliefMem也取得了最佳平均性能，显著优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 概率记忆范式为部分可观测环境下的智能体记忆探索了新的方向，通过保留不确定性使智能体能在高置信度证据上行动，同时保留更新置信度的能力。

Abstract: LLM agents that operate over long context depend on external memory to accumulate knowledge over time. However, existing methods typically store each observation as a single deterministic conclusion (e.g., inferring "API~X failed" from temporary errors), even though such observations are inherently partial and potentially ambiguous. By committing to one conclusion and discarding uncertainty, these methods introduce self-reinforcing error: the agent acts on the stored conclusion, never revisits alternatives, and reinforces the conclusion over time. To address this issue, we propose BeliefMem, which shifts the memory paradigm from committing to a single conclusion per observation to retaining multiple candidate conclusions with their probabilities. Concretely, BeliefMem stores the candidate conclusions as separate memory entries, each carrying a probability that is updated via Noisy-OR rules as new observations arrive. At retrieval, all candidates surface together with their probabilities, keeping alternatives visible to the agent. Since each conclusion in memory retains its probability, BeliefMem preserves the uncertainty that the deterministic paradigm discards, enabling the agent to act with high confidence on well-evidenced knowledge while retaining the capacity to update its confidence when new evidence arrives. Empirical evaluations on LoCoMo and ALFWorld benchmarks show that, even with limited data, BeliefMem achieves the best average performance, remarkably outperforming well-known baselines. More broadly, such probabilistic memory produces substantial gains and explores a new direction for agent memory in partially observable environments.

</details>


### [177] [Text-Graph Synergy: A Bidirectional Verification and Completion Framework for RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05643)
*Jiarui Zhong,Hong Cai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TGS-RAG是一个统一的文本-图协同增强框架，通过双向机制（Graph-to-Text的全局投票重排序和Text-to-Graph的记忆桥接算法）解决非对称推理导致的"信息孤岛"问题，在多跳推理基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本RAG检索伪证据，图RAG受剪枝限制丢失有效路径，现有混合方法仅简单拼接或单向增强，无法解决非对称推理流导致的"信息孤岛"问题。

Method: 提出TGS-RAG框架，包含两个双向通道：(i) Graph-to-Text通道采用全局投票策略对图节点进行重排序以过滤文本噪声；(ii) Text-to-Graph通道使用基于记忆的孤立实体桥接算法，利用文本线索从搜索历史中复活被剪枝的有效推理路径，无需额外数据库开销。

Result: 在多个多跳推理基准上，TGS-RAG显著优于现有最先进基线，在检索精度和计算效率之间取得了更优的平衡。

Conclusion: TGS-RAG通过文本与图的双向协同增强，有效解决了异构数据源间的信息孤岛问题，为RAG系统提供了一种更鲁棒且高效的统一框架。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a core paradigm for enhancing factual grounding and multi-hop reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs). Traditional text-based RAG often retrieves logically irrelevant pseudo-evidence, while graph-based RAG is frequently hindered by search-time pruning, which may discard potentially valid reasoning paths. Existing hybrid approaches primarily adopt simple evidence concatenation or unidirectional enhancement, which fails to address the fundamental "Information Island" problem caused by asymmetric reasoning flows between unstructured text and structured graphs. We propose \textbf{TGS-RAG}, a unified framework for \textbf{T}ext-\textbf{G}raph \textbf{S}ynergistic enhancement. TGS-RAG introduces a bidirectional mechanism: (i) a \textbf{Graph-to-Text} channel that employs a Global Voting strategy from visited graph nodes to re-rank and refine textual evidence, filtering out semantic noise; and (ii) a \textbf{Text-to-Graph} channel that utilizes the \textbf{Memory-based Orphan Entity Bridging} algorithm. This algorithm utilizes textual cues to proactively resurrect valid but previously pruned reasoning paths from the search history without additional database overhead. Experimental results on multiple multi-hop reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that TGS-RAG significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a superior balance between retrieval precision and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [178] [Large Vision-Language Models Get Lost in Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05668)
*Gongli Xi,Ye Tian,Mengyu Yang,Huahui Yi,Liang Lin,Xiaoshuai Hao,Kun Wang,Wendong Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于信息论和几何学的统一框架，用于量化残差更新的几何与熵特性，揭示LVLM中Attention模块是保持子空间的重新配置算子，FFN是扩展子空间的语义创新算子。实验发现用预设值（如高斯噪声）替换注意力权重在多数数据集上表现不逊甚至优于原始模型，说明当前注意力机制存在严重的资源错配和冗余，模型实际上是"迷失在注意力中"而非有效利用视觉上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于统计的归因方法缺乏统一理论基础，无法系统理解LVLM解码器中残差连接Transformer架构内部模块的不同作用。

Method: 提出一个基于信息论和几何学的统一框架来量化残差更新的几何属性和熵特性，对Attention和FFN模块进行功能解耦分析，并通过替换注意力权重为预设值进行验证实验。

Result: Attention是子空间保持的重新配置算子，FFN是子空间扩展的语义创新算子；用高斯噪声等预设值替换学习到的注意力权重在大多数数据集上表现相当或更优。

Conclusion: 当前SOTA LVLM存在严重的注意力机制资源错配和冗余，模型效果并非来自对视觉上下文的有效利用，而是"迷失在注意力"中。

Abstract: Despite the rapid evolution of training paradigms, the decoder backbone of large vision--language models (LVLMs) remains fundamentally rooted in the residual-connection Transformer architecture. Therefore, deciphering the distinct roles of internal modules is critical for understanding model mechanics and guiding architectural optimization. While prior statistical approaches have provided valuable attribution-based insights, they often lack a unified theoretical basis. To bridge this gap, we propose a unified framework grounded in information theory and geometry to quantify the geometric and entropic nature of residual updates. Applying this unified framework reveals a fundamental functional decoupling: Attention acts as a subspace-preserving operator focused on reconfiguration, whereas FFNs serve as subspace-expanding operators driving semantic innovation. Strikingly, further experiments demonstrate that replacing learned attention weights with predefined values (e.g., Gaussian noise) yields comparable or even superior performance across a majority of datasets relative to vanilla models. These results expose severe misallocation and redundancy in current mechanisms, suggesting that state-of-the-art LVLMs effectively ``get lost in attention'' rather than efficiently leveraging visual context.

</details>


### [179] [Attractor Geometry of Transformer Memory: From Conflict Arbitration to Confident Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05686)
*Qiyao Liang,Risto Miikkulainen,Ila Fiete*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文揭示了语言模型两种失败模式（冲突与幻觉）的统一几何解释：记忆事实构成吸引子盆地，冲突是盆地竞争，幻觉是盆地缺失。冻结的输出头无法区分这两种情况，总是自信输出。几何间隔比输出熵能更干净地分离正确召回与幻觉，且这种分离在自然语言任务中成立。自信幻觉遵循缩放定律，随模型规模增长而增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型依赖两种知识来源（参数记忆PM和上下文工作记忆WM），当两者冲突或知识缺失时会产生错误输出，且输出层无法区分这些情况。现有基于输出的监控方法（如熵检测）本质上无法有效识别这些失败，需要从内部机制理解其几何本质。

Method: 提出统一几何框架：在自回归生成隐状态空间中，已学习的事实形成吸引子盆地。通过合成任务（使用LoRA注入PM）和自然语言任务进行验证，利用几何间隔（隐状态到最近记忆盆地的距离）作为检测指标，并分析缩放定律。

Result: 几何间隔能零假拒绝地分离正确召回与幻觉，而熵检测会错误拒绝绝大多数正确输出。自信幻觉比例遵循C = exp(-c/Δ̄)缩放定律，随模型规模增长而增加。隐状态可靠编码认知状态，但冻结输出头系统性地抹除这些信息，且随规模增大而恶化。

Conclusion: 语言模型的失败模式源于隐藏状态空间中的几何结构（吸引子盆地），而非输出不确定性。需要从内部表示层面而非输出层面进行监控和干预，因为输出头会系统性地抹除认知状态信息，且这一缺陷随模型扩增而加剧。

Abstract: Language models draw on two knowledge sources: facts baked into weights (parametric memory, PM) and information in context (working memory, WM). We study two mechanistically distinct failure modes--conflict, when PM and WM disagree and interfere; and hallucination, when the queried fact was never learned. Both produce confident output regardless, making output-based monitoring blind by design. We show both failures share a unified geometric account. In the hidden-state space of autoregressive generation, learned facts form attractor basins. Conflict is basin competition: WM disrupts convergence to the correct basin without raising output entropy. Hallucination is basin absence: the hidden state drifts freely when no memorized basin exists. The frozen LM head, designed for next-token prediction, cannot distinguish these cases and fires confidently either way. We verify this account in a controlled synthetic task--entity identifiers mapped to unique codes with PM installed via LoRA adapters--where ground truth is exact and component roles can be causally isolated through targeted adapter placement. Geometric margin--the hidden state's distance to the nearest memorized basin--reads this geometry directly and separates correct recall from hallucination far more cleanly than output entropy, with zero false refusals where entropy-based detection cannot avoid rejecting the vast majority of correct outputs. The separation holds on natural-language factual queries from the pretrained model with no adaptation, confirming attractor geometry is structural rather than a fine-tuning artifact. The fraction of confident hallucinations follows a scaling law $C = \exp(-c/\barΔ)$, growing with scale even as overall error rates fall. Hidden states reliably encode epistemic state; the frozen output head systematically erases it--and this erasure worsens with scale.

</details>


### [180] [DataDignity: Training Data Attribution for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05687)
*Xiaomin Li,Andrzej Banburski-Fahey,Jaron Lanier*

Main category: cs.AI
